how the score worksscore per question = 0.85 × share of the must-have words found (0 if any must-not word is there) + 0.15 × the link, only for questions that need a link · total = Σ score × weight / Σ weight (the weight is under each question) · hover a score to see why

Dupaco offers the following Visa credit cards: **Personal Credit Cards:** * **Rewards Visa:** * Earns 1 point for every $1 spent, redeemable for cash back, travel, and more. * No annual fee. * Competitive rate if carrying a balance: 15.50%–21.00% APR. * **Platinum Visa:** * No annual fee. * Lower ongoing rate if carrying a balance: 12.50%–19.50% APR. * New cardmember offer: 2.90% intro APR on transferred balances for 6 months (after that, the rate is 12.50%–19.50% APR). **Business Credit Cards:** * **Business Platinum Visa:** * Variable Annual Percentage Rate (APR) of 15.50%. * Minimum credit limit of $500. * **Business Rewards Visa:** * Variable Annual Percentage Rate (APR) of 20.5%. * Minimum credit limit of $1,500. * Earns 1 point for every $1 spent, redeemable for cash back, merchandise, and more. For the Dupaco Platinum Visa, the 2.90% introductory APR on balance transfers is valid for new cardholders' balance transfers that post within the first 90 days following the opening of the card, and this introductory rate expires six months from the date of the first balance transfer, reverting to a variable rate of 12.50% APR to 19.50% APR based on creditworthiness.

What is the standard purchase APR on the Dupaco Platinum Visa?
12.5% standard APR (range up to 19.5%); 2.9% is only the intro balance-transfer APR for the first 90 days.
must have: 12.5 · must not: 9.9%
dup-035 · weight 1.5
script 1
The standard APR on the Dupaco Platinum Visa is a variable rate of 12.50% to 19.50%, based on your creditworthiness (effective 1/1/2026). The rate follows the Prime Rate published in The Wall Street Journal on the last business day of each calendar quarter, plus a margin set by your credit score at application. New cardholders can also get a 2.90% introductory APR on balance transfers for six months, after which the standard rate applies. There is no annual fee and a grace period on purchases.

Based on the Dupaco website excerpts provided, here is what you should know about construction loan interest rates: * **Interest Rate Type:** Dupaco offers a **one-year adjustable-rate mortgage (ARM)**, also known as a **1/1 ARM**. This means the interest rate is fixed for the first year. * **Rate Adjustments:** After the initial fixed year, your interest rate **can adjust up to 2% annually**. Furthermore, over the life of the loan, your interest rate will **stay within 6% of the original rate**. * **Payment Structure:** During the first 10 months of the construction phase, interest accrues on the money you withdraw, but **no payments are required**. At month 11, you pay the accrued interest, and full loan repayment begins at month 12. **Summary of Key Points:** * **Fixed Period:** 1 year * **Adjustment Cap:** Up to 2% annually after the first year * **Lifetime Cap:** Rate stays within 6% of the original rate * **Initial Payments:** No payments for the first 10 months; interest-only payment at month 11, full repayment starts month 12.

Over the life of the loan, your interest rate will stay within 6% of your original rate. *(Note: In general, interest rates on construction loans tend to be higher than traditional mortgages, though interest rates on both loans fluctuate.)*

script 1
You can update your personal information by logging into your account and making the changes. You can also call Dupaco at 800-373-7600, email service@dupaco.com, or visit a Dupaco branch in person. You may need to verify your identity and account ownership.
script 1runs 1 · 1 · 1
You can update your personal information in the following ways: * **Online:** Log into your account and make the desired changes to any accounts you have created with us. * **Phone:** Call Dupaco at 800-373-7600. * **Email:** Send an email to service@dupaco.com. * **In Person:** Visit a Dupaco branch in person to request access to or correction of any personal information you have provided. Please note that we may not be able to accommodate a request to change information if we cannot adequately verify your identity and account ownership, or if we believe the change would violate any law or legal requirement or cause the information to be incorrect.

You also need to notify your credit union by calling from the number listed on your account, especially when activating a Dupaco debit or Visa credit card. Additionally, you can review and change your personal information in any accounts you have created with Dupaco by logging into your account. Alternatively, you may call Dupaco at 800-373-7600, send an email to service@dupaco.com, or visit a Dupaco branch in person to request access to or correct any personal information you have provided.

What is Dupaco's Home$tart program?
Home$tart (FHLB) provides up to $15,000 toward down payment and closing costs for first-time homebuyers.
must have: 15,000
dup-031 · weight 1
script 1
Home$tart is a program Dupaco offers with the Federal Home Loan Bank (FHLB) of Des Moines to help first-time homebuyers. It gives eligible buyers up to $15,000 in assistance, applied as a credit toward the down payment and closing costs. To learn more and apply, visit dupaco.com/firsthome.

Dupaco is partnering with the Federal Home Loan Bank (FHLB) of Des Moines to offer the Home$tart program to help first-time homebuyers. This initiative provides up to $15,000 in assistance, which is applied as a credit toward down payment and closing costs for eligible buyers beginning their homeownership journey.

At Dupaco, borrowers take out a one-year adjustable-rate mortgage (ARM), also called a 1/1 ARM. Your interest rate stays the same for the first year, but it can adjust up to 2% annually after that. Over the life of the loan, your interest rate will stay within 6% of the original rate.

script 0runs 0 · 1 · 0
At Dupaco, borrowers take out a one-year adjustable-rate mortgage (ARM), also called a 1/1 ARM, for a construction loan. Your interest rate stays the same for the first year (or during the first 10-month construction period, where interest only accrues on the money you withdraw). After that, your rate can adjust up to 2% annually (or every 12 months), and over the life of the loan, your interest rate will stay within 6% of the original rate. Additionally, in general, interest rates on construction loans tend to be higher than traditional mortgages.

I'm a student going to a new university - do you have any loan options with flexibility?
Describe student loans (new or refinance, flexible repayment, no origination fees).
must have: student loan · must not: Share Savings, 0.20%
dup-101 · weight 1.5
script 1
Yes. Dupaco offers student loans with flexibility: - Gap funding for costs federal aid doesn't cover (tuition, lab fees, textbooks, living expenses). - One-time application that covers your entire undergraduate career. - Flexible repayment: defer full payments while in school, and choose flexible terms, including graduated repayment after graduation. - $0 origination fees and no prepayment penalties. You can apply at apply.studentchoice.org/application.
script 1runs 1 · 1 · 1
Yes, we have flexible options for student loans and refinancing to help you take your education higher. Our student loan program offers: * **Gap Funding:** Cover costs that federal aid leaves behind, such as tuition, lab fees, textbooks, and daily living expenses. * **One-Time Application:** Apply just once and secure funding for your entire undergraduate career without needing to reapply every year. * **Flexible Repayment:** You can defer full payments while in school and choose from flexible terms, including graduated repayment options after graduation. * **Keep More Money:** Benefit from $0 origination fees, no prepayment penalties, and favorable interest rates lower than many commercial lenders.

Home$tart is a program Dupaco offers with the Federal Home Loan Bank of Des Moines for first-time homebuyers. It gives eligible buyers up to $15,000 in assistance, applied as a credit toward down payment and closing costs. To learn more and apply, visit dupaco.com/firsthome.

At Dupaco, borrowers take out a one-year adjustable-rate mortgage (1/1 ARM), which means the interest rate you pay on your loan balance can vary over the life of the loan. Your interest rate stays the same for the first year, but it can adjust up to 2% annually after that (the rate will stay within 6% of the original rate over the life of the loan).

script 0runs 0 · 0 · 0
At Dupaco, borrowers take out a one-year adjustable-rate mortgage (ARM), also called a 1/1 ARM, for a construction loan. Your interest rate stays the same for the first year, and interest only accrues on the money you withdraw. After that, your rate can adjust up to 2% annually (or every 12 months), and over the life of the loan, your interest rate will stay within 6% of the original rate. (In general, interest rates on construction loans tend to be higher than traditional mortgages.)

Dupaco's 30-year 7/1 ARM has an initial interest rate that stays the same for the first seven years. After that, the rate adjusts annually for the remaining 23 years of the loan. During each periodic adjustment, the rate won't change more than 2%, and over the life of the loan, it won't change more than 6% from the original rate.

At Dupaco, borrowers take out a one-year adjustable-rate mortgage (1/1 ARM), which means the interest rate you pay on your loan balance can vary over the life of the loan. Your interest rate stays the same for the first year, but it can adjust up to 2% annually after that (the rate will stay within 6% of the original rate over the life of the loan).

At Dupaco, borrowers take out a one-year adjustable-rate mortgage (ARM), also called a 1/1 ARM, for a construction loan. Dupaco offers several credit card options with different rates: **Personal Credit Cards:** * **Platinum Visa:** The standard APR is 12.50% – 19.50%. New cardholders can get an introductory balance transfer rate of 2.90% for the first 90 days, which reverts to the standard APR after six months. There are no annual or participation fees. Other fees include a cash advance fee of $10.00 or 3.00% of the amount (whichever is greater), a foreign transaction fee of 1.00% of US dollar transactions, a late payment fee of $15 or the minimum due (whichever is less) if four or more days late, and a returned payment fee up to $25. * **Rewards Visa:** The standard APR is 15.50% – 21.00%. There is no annual fee or balance transfer fee. Other fees include a cash advance fee of $10.00 or 3.00% of the amount (whichever is greater), a foreign transaction fee of 1.00% of US dollar transactions, a late payment fee up to $15.00, and a returned payment fee up to $25.00. **Business Credit Cards:** * **Business Platinum Visa:** The APR is 15.50%. The interest rate is variable, determined by the Prime Rate plus a margin, and will never be less than 12.00% or exceed 21.00%. There is no annual fee. Other fees include a cash advance fee of $10.00 or 3.00% of the amount (whichever is greater), a foreign transaction fee of 1.00% of US dollar transactions, a late payment fee up to $35.00, and a returned payment fee up to $25.00. * **Business Rewards Visa:** The APR is 20.50%. The interest rate is variable, determined by the Prime Rate plus a margin, and will never be less than 17.00% or exceed 21.00%. There is no annual fee. Other fees include a cash advance fee of $10.00 or 3.00% of the amount (whichever is greater), a foreign transaction fee of 1.00% of US dollar transactions, a late payment fee up to $35.00, and a returned payment fee up to $25.00.

What is Dupaco's Home$tart program?
Home$tart (FHLB) provides up to $15,000 toward down payment and closing costs for first-time homebuyers.

script 1
Home$tart is a program for first-time homebuyers that Dupaco offers with the Federal Home Loan Bank of Des Moines. It gives eligible buyers up to $15,000, applied as a credit toward the down payment and closing costs. You can learn more and apply at dupaco.com/firsthome.

The program provides up to $15,000 in assistance, which is applied as a credit toward down payments and closing costs for eligible buyers beginning their homeownership journey.

Dupaco's Home$tart program is a partnership with the Federal Home Loan Bank (FHLB) of Des Moines that provides up to $15,000 in assistance applied as a credit toward down payment and closing costs for eligible first-time homebuyers. The program aims to help first-time buyers take the leap into homeownership by removing financial barriers. According to the excerpt, first-time buyers made up over 54% of Dupaco's primary residence mortgages in 2025, and the program provides personalized guidance along with financial assistance. More information can be found at dupaco.com/firsthome.

At Dupaco, construction loans use a one-year adjustable-rate mortgage (ARM) / 1/1 ARM. Your interest rate stays the same (fixed) for the first year. After that, it can adjust up to 2% annually (or every 12 months), but over the life of the loan, your interest rate will always stay within 6% of your original rate.

script 1runs 1 · 1 · 1
You can update your personal information by logging into your account and making the desired changes directly. Alternatively, you can call Dupaco at 800-373-7600, send an email to service@dupaco.com, or visit a Dupaco branch in person to request access to or corrections to your information. If you are moving, it is especially important to notify the credit union when activating your Dupaco debit or Visa credit cards; when doing so, you should call from the number listed on your account.
script 1
You can update your personal information through the following methods: * **Online:** Log into your account and make the desired changes directly. * **Phone:** Call Dupaco at 800-373-7600. * **Email:** Send an email to service@dupaco.com. * **In Person:** Visit a Dupaco branch. Please note that we may not be able to accommodate a request to change information if we cannot adequately verify your identity and account ownership, or if the change would violate legal requirements or cause the information to be incorrect. Additionally, if you are moving and activating a Dupaco debit or Visa credit card, you should call from the number listed on your account.

I'm a student going to a new university - do you have any loan options with flexibility?
Describe student loans (new or refinance, flexible repayment, no origination fees).
must have: student loan · must not: Share Savings, 0.20%
dup-101 · weight 1.5
script 1
Yes. Dupaco offers student loans (and refinancing) with flexible repayment: you can defer full payments while in school and choose flexible terms, including graduated repayment after graduation. They cover costs federal aid leaves behind (tuition, lab fees, textbooks, living expenses), need just one application for your entire undergraduate career, and have $0 origination fees and no prepayment penalties. Apply at apply.studentchoice.org/application.

Yes, Dupaco offers student loan options with flexibility. Our student loans are designed to help you fill the funding gap for college and include flexible repayment options, such as the ability to defer full payments while you are in school and the choice of flexible terms, including graduated repayment options after graduation.
script 1
Yes, Dupaco offers student loan options with several flexible features to help you: * **Flexible Repayment:** You can defer full payments while you are in school and choose from various terms, including graduated repayment options after you graduate. * **One-Time Application:** You can apply just once to secure funding for your entire undergraduate career, meaning you do not need to reapply every year. * **Gap Funding:** These loans can cover costs that federal aid may leave behind, such as tuition, lab fees, textbooks, and daily living expenses. You can apply for a student loan or refinancing at apply.studentchoice.org/application.
